for a pair of distributions  $p \ll q$  with densities  $p$  and  $q$ , we define

$$
d _ {\mathrm {k l}} (p \parallel q) = \int p (x) \log (p (x) / q (x)) d x.
$$

lemma b.4 (high-probability pinsker (lemma 2.6, tsybakov (2008))). let  $p$  and  $q$  be probability measures over a measurable space  $(\omega, \mathcal{f})$ . then for any measurable subset  $a \in \mathcal{f}$ ,

$$
p (a) + q \left(a ^ {c}\right) \geq \frac {1}{2} \exp \left(- d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(p \| q\right)\right). \tag {42}
$$

for  $p\in [0,1]$ , we let  $\operatorname{ber}(p)$  denote the bernoulli distribution over  $\{0,1\}$  with bias  $\mathbb{p}(x = 1) = p$ .

lemma b.5. for any  $p,q\in (0,1)$ ,  $d_{\mathrm{kl}}(\mathrm{ber}(p)\parallel \mathrm{ber}(q))\leq \frac{1}{\min\{p,q,1 - p,1 - q\}} |p - q|^2$

# c proofs for upper bounds

this section is dedicated to the proof of theorem 2.1 and theorem 2.3. for a brief overview of the proof ideas, we refer the reader to section 2.6.1.

for simplicity, in this section, we assume that algorithm 1 exactly computes  $\mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)$  and  $w(x;\mathcal{f}_m)$  for all  $x\in \mathcal{x}$  and all  $m$  (rather than approximately, using the oracle machinery of section 4). while we specify  $\delta = 1 / t$  in the pseudocode of algorithm 1, throughout this section we deal with a general value  $\delta \in (0,1]$ . likewise, we analyze a slightly more general version of the update in line 5, which sets the learning rate as

$$
\gamma_ {m} = \lambda_ {m} \cdot c \sqrt {\frac {a n _ {m - 1}}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}},
$$

where  $c > 0$  is an additional hyperparameter. we assume throughout the section that  $\{\beta_m\}_{m=1}^m \in \mathbb{r}_+^m$ ,  $\{\mu_m\}_{m=1}^m \in \mathbb{r}_+^m$ .

# c.1 preliminaries

for all  $t \in [t]$ , we let  $\mathfrak{g}_t = \sigma((x_1, a_1, r_1(a_1)), \ldots, (x_t, a_t, r_t(a_t)))$  denote the sigma-algebra generated by the history up to round  $t$  (inclusive), and let  $m(t) := \min\{m \in \mathbb{n} : t \leq \tau_m\}$  denote the epoch that round  $t$  belongs to; recall that epoch  $m$  consists of rounds  $\tau_{m-1} + 1, \ldots, \tau_m$ . we define

$$
\overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} := \sum_ {t = 1} ^ {t} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right]
$$

to be the sum of conditional expectations of the instantaneous regret.

algorithm-related definitions. for all epoch  $m \in [m]$ , for all round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , we define the following quantities, all of which are  $\mathfrak{g}_{\tau_{m-1}}$ -measurable. first, we define the greedy policy for epoch  $m$  by

$$
\forall x \in \mathcal {x}, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) := \arg \max  _ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, a).
$$

next, we denote the algorithm's probability distribution for epoch  $m$  by

$$
\forall x \in \mathcal {x}, p _ {m} (a \mid x) := \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {1}{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | + \gamma_ {m} \big (\widehat {f} _ {m} (x , \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x , a) \big)}, & \text {f o r a l l} a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) / \{\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) \}, \\ 1 - \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) / \{\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) \}} p _ {m} (a \mid x), & \text {f o r} a = \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x), \\ 0, & \text {f o r} a \notin \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}). \end{array} \right.
$$

finally, we define the following disagreement-related quantities:

$$
q _ {m} := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} [ \mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x, \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \} ] = \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1).
$$

$$
\widehat {q} _ {m} := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d} _ {m}} [ \mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x, \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \} ] = \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d} _ {m}} (| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1).
$$

$$
\varrho_ {m} := q _ {m} + \mu_ {m}.
$$

$$
\widehat {\varrho} _ {m} := \widehat {q} _ {m} + \mu_ {m}.
$$

$$
w _ {m} := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} [ w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) ].
$$

$$
\widehat {w} _ {m} := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d} _ {m}} [ w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) ].
$$

policy-related definitions. we define the universal policy space (simchi-levi and xu, 2020) as  $\psi \coloneqq \mathcal{a}^{\mathcal{x}}$ , and for all  $\pi \in \psi$  we define

$$
\mathcal {r} (\pi) := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi (x)) \right] \quad \text {a n d} \quad \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) := \mathcal {r} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) - \mathcal {r} (\pi).
$$

for all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , we define the following quantities for all  $\pi \in \psi$ :

$$
\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} (\pi) := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \widehat {f} _ {m (t)} (x, \pi (x)) \right].
$$

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) := \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} \left(\widehat {\pi} _ {m (t)}\right) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} (\pi).
$$

$$
\mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} [ \mathbb {i} \left\{\left| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \right\} f ^ {\star} (x, \pi (x)) ].
$$

$$
\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) := \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \} \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x)) \right].
$$

high-probability events. the proofs in this section involve three high-probability events  $\mathcal{e}$ ,  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$ , and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}$  which are defined in lemma c.1, lemma c.3 and lemma c.4 below. we also present lemma c.2, which is a consequence of the event  $\mathcal{e}$  stated in lemma c.1.

lemma c.1 (lemma 9, foster et al. (2018)). let  $c_{\delta} \coloneqq 16 \log \left(\frac{2|\mathcal{f}|t^2}{\delta}\right)$ . define

$$
m _ {t} (f) := \left(f \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {2} - \left(f ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {2}.
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta /2$ , it holds that

$$
\sum_ {t = \tau} ^ {\tau^ {\prime}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t}, a _ {t}} \left[ \left(f \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] = \sum_ {t = \tau} ^ {\tau^ {\prime}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t}, a _ {t}} \left[ m _ {t} (f) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \leq 2 \sum_ {t = \tau} ^ {\tau^ {\prime}} m _ {t} (f) + c _ {\delta}
$$

for all  $f \in \mathcal{f}$  and  $\tau, \tau' \in [t]$ .

we let  $\mathcal{e}$  denote the high-probability event from lemma c.1. we have the following consequence.

lemma c.2. assume that  $\mathcal{e}$  holds. then

1. for all  $m\in [m]$  , for all  $\beta_{m}\geq 0$

$$
\sum_ {t = \tau_ {m - 2} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t}, a _ {t}} \left[ \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \leq c _ {\delta}, \tag {43}
$$

and

$$
\forall f \in \mathcal {f} _ {m}, \quad \sum_ {t = \tau_ {m - 2} + 1} ^ {t _ {m - 1}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t}, a _ {t}} \left[ \left(f \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}, a _ {t}\right)\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \leq 2 \beta_ {m} + c _ {\delta}.
$$

2. if  $\beta_{m} \geq c_{\delta} / 2$  for all  $m \in [m]$ , then  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_{m}$  for all  $m \in [m]$ .

3. if  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ , then  $f^{\star}\in \mathcal{f}_m\subset \mathcal{f}_{m - 1}\subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$

proof. part 1 follows from lemma c.1 and the fact that  $\widehat{f}_m$  minimizes the empirical square loss. parts 2 and 3 are adapted from lemma 10 of foster et al. (2018).

lemma c.3. for any  $\delta \in (0,1]$ , if we set  $\mu_{m} = 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ , then with probability at least  $1 - \delta /2$ , the following event holds:

$$
\mathcal {e} _ {\mathrm {d p}} := \left\{\forall m \in [ m ], \quad \frac {2}{3} \varrho_ {m} \leq \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} \leq \frac {4}{3} \varrho_ {m} \right\}. \tag {44}
$$

proof. for  $m = 1$ ,  $\varrho_{m} = q_{1} + 128\log (4m / \delta)$ ,  $\widehat{\varrho}_{m} = 1 + 128\log (4m / \delta)$ , so  $\frac{2}{3}\varrho_{1} \leq \widehat{\varrho}_{1} \leq \frac{4}{3}\varrho_{1}$  trivially holds.

fix any  $m \in [m] \setminus \{1\}$ . since  $x_{t_{m-1}+1}, \ldots, x_{\tau_{m-1}}$  are independent of  $\mathcal{f}_m$ , we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t} \sim \mathcal {d}} [ \mathbb {i} \left\{\left| \mathcal {a} (x _ {t}; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \right\} ] = \mathbb {p} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left(\left| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1\right) = q _ {m}
$$

for  $t = t_{m - 1} + 1,\ldots ,\tau_{m - 1}$  . thus given  $\mathcal{f}_m$ $\mathbb{i}\{|a(x_{t_{m - 1} + 1};\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1\} ,\dots ,\mathbb{i}\{|a(x_{\tau_{m - 1}};\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1\}$  are  $n_{m - 1} / 2$  i.i.d. [0,1]-valued random variable with mean  $q_{m}$  . since  $\widehat{q}_{m} = \frac{1}{n_{m - 1} / 2}\sum_{t = t_{m - 1} + 1}^{\tau_{m - 1}}\mathbb{i}\{|a(x_t;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1\}$  , by lemma b.3, we know that

$$
\left| \widehat {q} _ {m} - q _ {m} \right| \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {q _ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \tag {45}
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta /(2m)$ . note that to apply lemma b.3, we have used that our sample splitting schedule guarantees that the contexts  $x_{t_{m - 1} + 1},\ldots ,\tau_{m - 1}$  used to form  $\widehat{q}_m$  are independent of  $\mathcal{f}_m$ . continuing, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \varrho_ {m} - \varrho_ {m} | = | \widehat {q} _ {m} - q _ {m} | \\ \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {q _ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \\ \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {\varrho_ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \\ \leq \frac {2}{3} \frac {\varrho_ {m}}{6 4} + 2 \frac {\varrho_ {m}}{8} \\ \leq \frac {1}{3} \varrho_ {m}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from (45), the second inequality follows from  $\varrho_{m} = q_{m} + \mu_{m}\geq q_{m}$ , and the third inequality follows from  $\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}\leq \mu_m / 64\leq \varrho_m / 64$

by a union bound over  $m \in [m]$ , this implies that with probability at least  $1 - \delta / 2$ ,

$$
\forall m \in [ m ], \quad \frac {2}{3} \varrho_ {m} \leq \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} \leq \frac {4}{3} \varrho_ {m}.
$$

![](images/3cee523241945cdade81fec555d63314ca2750f3619411d488d9047ba253ef16.jpg)

lemma c.4. let  $\delta \in (0,1]$  be fixed. for each  $m\in [m]$ , let  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}^{(m)}$  denote the event that

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \widehat {w} _ {m} \in [ \frac {2}{3} w _ {m}, \frac {4}{3} w _ {m} ], & \text {i f} w _ {m} \geq 6 4 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}, \\ \widehat {w} _ {m} <   6 5 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}, & \text {i f} w _ {m} <   6 4 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}. \end{array} \right. \tag {46}
$$

let  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}} = \bigcap_{m=1}^{m} \mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}^{(m)}$ . then with probability at least  $1 - \delta/2$ ,  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}$  holds.

proof. for  $m = 1$ ,  $w_{m} \leq 1 < 64 \log(4m / \delta) / n_{m-1}$ , and we likewise do have  $\widehat{w}_{m} \leq 1 \leq 64 \log(4m / \delta) / n_{m-1}$ . fix any  $m \in [m] \setminus \{1\}$ . since  $x_{t_{m-1}+1}, \ldots, x_{\tau_{m-1}}$  are independent of  $\mathcal{f}_{m}$ , we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x _ {t} \sim \mathcal {d}} [ w (x _ {t}; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \} ] = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} [ w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) ] = w _ {m}
$$

for  $t = t_{m-1} + 1, \ldots, \tau_{m-1}$ . thus given  $\mathcal{f}_m$ ,  $w(x_{t_{m-1}+1}; \mathcal{f}_m), \ldots, w(x_{\tau_{m-1}}; \mathcal{f}_m)$  are i.i.d. [0, 1]-valued random variables with mean  $q_m$ . since  $\widehat{q}_m = \frac{1}{n_{m-1}/2} \sum_{t=t_{m-1}+1}^{\tau_{m-1}} w(x_t; \mathcal{f}_m)$ , by lemma b.3, we know that

$$
\left| \widehat {w} _ {m} - w _ {m} \right| \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {w _ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \tag {47}
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta /(2m)$ . if  $w_{m}\geq 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$ , then

$$
| \widehat {w} _ {m} - w _ {m} | \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {w _ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \leq \frac {2}{3} \frac {w _ {m}}{6 4} + 2 \frac {w _ {m}}{8} \leq \frac {1}{3} w _ {m},
$$

where the first inequality follows from (47) and the second inequality follows from  $\log(4m/\delta)/n_{m-1} \leq w_m/64$ . in this case,  $2/3w_m \leq \widehat{w}_m \leq 4/3w_m$ . if  $w_m < 64\log(4m/\delta)/n_{m-1}$ , then

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \widehat {w} _ {m} - w _ {m} | \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + 2 \sqrt {\frac {w _ {m} \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}} \\ \leq \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{3 n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {2 \log (4 m / \delta)}{8 n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {1 1}{1 2} \frac {\log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from (47) and the second inequality follows from  $w_{m} \leq \log(4m / \delta) / n_{m-1}$ . in this case,  $\widehat{w}_{m} \leq w_{m} + \log(4m / \delta) / n_{m-1} < 65\log(4m / \delta) / n_{m-1}$ .

by a union bound over  $m \in [m]$ , we know that with probability at least  $1 - \delta / 2$ ,

$$
\forall m \in [ m ], \quad \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \widehat {w} _ {m} \in [ \frac {2}{3} w _ {m}, \frac {4}{3} w _ {m} ], & \text {i f} w _ {m} \geq 6 4 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}, \\ \widehat {w} _ {m} <   6 5 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}, & \text {i f} w _ {m} <   6 4 \log (4 m / \delta) / n _ {m - 1}. \end{array} \right.
$$

# c.2 analysis in policy space

as mentioned in section 2.6.1, our regret analysis builds on a framework established in simchi-levi and xu (2020), which analyzes contextual bandit algorithms in the universal policy space  $\psi$ . in this section, we prove a number of structural properties for the action distribution  $p_m$  selected in algorithm 1 by focusing on a data-dependent subspace of  $\psi$  at each epoch (this is closely related to the elimination procedure of our algorithm and is essential to our instance-dependent analysis).

the analysis in this subsection deals with an arbitrary choice for the scale factor schedule  $\{\lambda_m\}_{m=1}^m$  rather than the explicit specification in algorithm 1, and serves as a foundation of the proofs of the main theorems in appendix c.3 and appendix c.4 (where we instantiate the learning rate using option i and option ii).

for each epoch  $m \in [m]$  and any round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , for any possible realization of  $\gamma_m$ ,  $\widehat{f}_m$  and  $\mathcal{f}_m$ , we define a (data-dependent) subspace of  $\psi$ :

$$
\psi_ {m} := \prod_ {x} \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}).
$$

let  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  be the equivalent policy distribution for  $p_{m}(\cdot \mid \cdot)$ , i.e.,

$$
q _ {m} (\pi) := \prod_ {x} p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x), \forall \pi \in \psi .
$$

note that both  $\psi_{m}$  and  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  are  $\mathfrak{g}_{\tau_{m-1}}$ -measurable. we refer to section 3.2 of simchi-levi and xu (2020) for more detailed intuition for  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  and proof of existence. by lemma 4 of simchi-levi and xu (2020), we know that for all epoch  $m \in [m]$  and all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ ,

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) - r _ {t} (a _ {t}) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] = \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) - r _ {t} (a _ {t}) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \right] = \sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi).
$$

moreover, since the specification of  $p_m(\cdot \mid \cdot)$  ensures that  $q_{m}(\pi) = 0$  for all  $\pi \notin \psi_{m}$ , we know that  $\sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_{m}(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi) = \sum_{\pi \in \psi_{m}}q_{m}(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi)$ , thus it is sufficient for us to only focus on policies in  $\psi_{m}$  at epoch  $m$ . the following lemma is an extension of lemma 5 and 6 of simchi-levi and xu (2020), which refines the policy space from  $\psi$  to  $\psi_{m}$ .

lemma c.5 (implicit optimization problem). for all epochs  $m \in [m]$  and all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ ,  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  is a feasible solution to the following implicit optimization problem:

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) \leq \left(\mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | \right] - 1\right) / \gamma_ {m}, \tag {48}
$$

$$
\forall \pi \in \psi_ {m}, \quad \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | \right] + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi). \tag {49}
$$

proof. let  $m$  and  $t$  in epoch  $m$  be fixed. we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) = \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x)) \right] \\ = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x))\right) \right] \\ = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} \mathbb {i} \{\pi (x) = a \} q _ {m} (\pi) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, a)\right) \right] \\ = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} p _ {m} (a \mid x) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, a)\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

now, given any context  $x \in \mathcal{x}$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} p _ {m} (a \mid x) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, a)\right) = \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \backslash \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)} \frac {\widehat {f} _ {m} (x , \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x , a)}{| \mathcal {a} (x , \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | + \gamma_ {m} \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x , \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x , a)\right)} \\ \leq \frac {| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | - 1}{\gamma_ {m}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

the result in (48) follows immediately by taking an expectation over  $x \sim \mathcal{d}$ .

for (49), we first observe that for any policy  $\pi \in \psi_{m}$ , given any context  $x\in \mathcal{x}$

$$
\frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} = | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | + \gamma_ {m} \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x))\right), \quad \mathrm {i f} \pi (x) \neq \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x),
$$

and

$$
\frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \leq \frac {1}{1 / | \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) |} = \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) + \gamma_ {m} \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x))\right), \quad \mathrm {i f} \pi (x) = \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x).
$$

thus

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | ] + \gamma_ {m} \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x)) - \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x)) \right] \right. \\ = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | \right] + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\mathrm {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi). \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/d1c1fadd30d4d3b83b99370a4eaac0887497bf8203643359f618af7d35be99f7.jpg)

we now formulate a more refined disagreement-based version of the implicit optimization problem.

lemma c.6 (disagreement-based implicit optimization problem). for all epochs  $m \in [m]$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ ,  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  is a feasible solution to the following constraints:

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) \leq q _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}, \tag {50}
$$

$$
\forall \pi \in \psi_ {m}: \quad \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq q _ {m} a + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi). \tag {51}
$$

the constraints (50) and (51) involve several data-dependent quantities including the disagreement indicator  $\mathbb{i}\{|a(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1\}$  and the disagreement probability  $q_{m}$ , and will play important roles in our analysis.

proof. fix epoch  $m$  and round  $t$ . (50) directly follows from (48). we now show that (51) holds. for any  $\pi \in \psi_{m}$ , we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] = \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {1 - \mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| = 1 \}}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right].
$$

note that when the indicator above is 1, we have  $p_m(\pi(x) \mid x) = 1$  for all  $\pi \in \psi_m$ , and hence this is equal to

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] - \mathbb {p} \{| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | = 1 \} \\ \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x} [ | \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | ] + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) - (1 - q _ {m}) \\ \leq \left(\left(1 - q _ {m}\right) + q _ {m} a\right) + \gamma_ {m} \widetilde {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) - \left(1 - q _ {m}\right) \\ = q _ {m} a + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi), \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from (49).

lemma c.7. assume that  $\mathcal{e}$  holds and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m$  for all  $m \in [m]$ . for all epochs  $m \in [m]$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and all policies  $\pi \in \psi_m$ , we have

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) = \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi), \quad \mathrm {a n d} \quad \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) = \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi).
$$

proof. since  $\widehat{f}_m \in \mathcal{f}_m$  and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m$ , for all  $\pi \in \psi_m$ , if  $|\mathcal{a}(x; \mathcal{f}_m)| = 1$ , then  $\pi(x) = \pi_{f^{\star}}(x) = \widehat{\pi}_m(x)$ . the result follows immediately from this observation.

lemma c.8. assume that  $\mathcal{e}$  holds and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . for all epochs  $m > 1$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and all policies  $\pi \in \psi_m$ , if  $\gamma_m > 0$ , then

$$
\left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) \right| \leq 4 c \sqrt {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right]} \frac {\lambda_ {m} \sqrt {a}}{\gamma_ {m}}.
$$

proof. fix any epoch  $m > 1$ , any round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and any policy  $\pi \in \psi_{m}$ . by the definitions of  $\widehat{\mathcal{r}}_t^{\mathrm{dis}}(\pi)$  and  $\mathcal{r}_t^{\mathrm{dis}}(\pi)$ , we have

$$
\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \} \left(\widehat {f _ {m}} (x, \pi (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi (x))\right) \right].
$$

given a context  $x$ , define

$$
\delta_ {x} = \widehat {f} _ {m} (x, \pi (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi (x))
$$

so that

$$
\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) = \mathbb {e} _ {x} [ \mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \} \delta_ {x} ].
$$

for all  $s = t_{m-2} + 1, \ldots, t_{m-1}$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} _ {a _ {s} | x _ {s}} \left[ \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x _ {s}, a _ {s}) - f ^ {\star} (x _ {s}, a _ {s})\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {s - 1} \right] = \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} _ {m - 1}} p _ {m - 1} (a \mid x _ {s}) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x _ {s}, a) - f ^ {\star} (x _ {s}, a)\right) ^ {2} \\ \geq p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x _ {s}) | x _ {s}) \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x _ {s}, \pi (x _ {s})) - f ^ {\star} (x _ {s}, \pi (x _ {s}))\right) ^ {2} \\ = p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi \left(x _ {s}\right) \mid x _ {s}\right) \left(\delta_ {x _ {s}}\right) ^ {2}. \tag {52} \\ \end{array}
$$

thus, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} e _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \cdot c _ {\delta} \geq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \cdot c _ {\delta} \\ \geq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \sum_ {s = \tau_ {m - 2} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {s}, a _ {s}} \left[ \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x _ {s}, a _ {s}) - f ^ {\star} (x _ {s}, a _ {s})\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {s - 1} \right] \\ = \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \sum_ {s = \tau_ {m - 2} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {s}} \mathbb {e} _ {a _ {s} \mid x _ {s}} \left[ \left(\widehat {f} _ {m} (x _ {s}, a _ {s}) - f ^ {\star} (x _ {s}, a _ {s})\right) ^ {2} \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {s - 1} \right] \\ \geq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \sum_ {s = \tau_ {m - 2} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \mathbb {e} _ {x _ {s}} \left[ p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x _ {s}) | x _ {s}) (\delta_ {x _ {s}}) ^ {2} \right] \\ = n _ {m - 1} \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x) (\delta_ {x}) ^ {2} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from  $\mathcal{f}_m\subset \mathcal{f}_{m - 1}$ , the second inequality follows from (43), and the third inequality follows from (52). to proceed, note that we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \left\{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \right\}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x) \left(\delta_ {x}\right) ^ {2} \right] \geq \left(\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \sqrt {\frac {\mathbb {i} \left\{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \right\}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x) \left(\delta_ {x}\right) ^ {2}} \right]\right) ^ {2} \\ = \left(\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ | \mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right| > 1 \} \delta_ {x} | \right]\right) ^ {2} \\ \geq n _ {m - 1} \left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) \right| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from cauchy-schwarz and the second inequality follows uses convexity of the  $l_{1}$  norm. now, from the definition of  $\gamma_{m}$ , we have

$$
\frac {c ^ {2} \lambda_ {m} ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} ^ {2}} = \frac {\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}} = \frac {c _ {\delta}}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \hat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) \right| ^ {2} \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \frac {c _ {\delta}}{n _ {m - 1}} \\ = 1 6 c ^ {2} \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\vert \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \vert > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \frac {\lambda_ {m} ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} ^ {2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

which concludes the proof.

# c.3 proof of theorem 2.1

we now prove theorem 2.1, which concerns adacb with option i, where

$$
\lambda_ {m} = \frac {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}{\sqrt {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}}}
$$

for all  $m \in [m]$  (for  $m = 1$ , we have defined  $\lambda_1 = 1$ ). note that since  $\lambda_{m} > 0$  for all  $m \in [m]$ , we have  $\gamma_{m} > 0$  for all  $m \in [m]$ . we consider general values for  $\{\beta_m\}_{m=1}^m$  unless explicitly specified.

lemma c.9. assume that  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  holds and  $\mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . then  $\gamma_m / \widehat{\varrho}_m$  is monotonically non-decreasing in  $m$ , i.e.,  $\gamma_1 / \widehat{\varrho}_1 \leq \dots \leq \gamma_m / \widehat{\varrho}_m$ .

proof. we have  $\frac{\gamma_1}{\widehat{\varrho}_1} = c\frac{1}{\widehat{\varrho}_1}\sqrt{\frac{a/2}{\log(2|\mathcal{f}|t^2/\delta)}}$  (since  $\lambda_1 = 1$ ) and

$$
\frac {\gamma_ {m}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} = c \frac {1}{\sqrt {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}}} \sqrt {\frac {a n _ {m - 1}}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}}
$$

for all  $m\in [m]\setminus \{1\}$ . since  $\widehat{\varrho}_{1} = \widehat{q}_{1} + \mu_{1}\geq \widehat{q}_{1} = 1$ , we have

$$
\frac {\gamma_ {1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {1}} = c \frac {1}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {1}} \sqrt {\frac {a / 2}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}} \leq c \frac {1}{\sqrt {\widehat {\varrho} _ {1}}} \sqrt {\frac {a}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}} = \frac {\gamma_ {2}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {2}}.
$$

for all  $m\in [m]\setminus \{1,2\}$  , we have

$$
\frac {\gamma_ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} \big / \frac {\gamma_ {m}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} = \sqrt {\frac {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 2}} \frac {n _ {m - 2}}{n _ {m - 1}}} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} \frac {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 2}}} \leq \sqrt {\frac {1}{2} \frac {\frac {4}{3} \varrho_ {m - 1}}{\frac {2}{3} \varrho_ {m - 2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {\varrho_ {m - 1}}{\varrho_ {m - 2}}} \leq 1,
$$

where the first inequality follows from (44) and the second inequality follows from  $\varrho_{m - 1} = q_{m - 1} + \mu_{m - 1}\leq$ $q_{m - 2} + \mu_{m - 2} = \varrho_{m - 2}$  (since  $\mathcal{f}_m\subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$

lemma c.10. assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold, and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . let  $c_1 \coloneqq 200c^2 + 3$ . for all epochs  $m \in [m]$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and all policies  $\pi \in \psi_m$ ,

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq 2 \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) + c _ {1} \varrho_ {m} a / \gamma_ {m},
$$

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) \leq 2 \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

proof. we prove lemma c.10 via induction on  $m$ . we first consider the base case where  $m = 1$  and  $1 \leq t \leq \tau_1$ . in this case, since  $\widehat{q}_1 = 1$  and  $\gamma_1 = c\sqrt{\frac{a/2}{\log(2|\mathcal{f}|t^2/\delta)}},$  we know that  $\forall \pi \in \psi_1$ ,

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq 1 = \widehat {q} _ {1} \leq \widehat {\varrho} _ {1} \leq c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {1} a / \gamma_ {1},
$$

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) \leq 1 = \widehat {q} _ {1} \leq \widehat {\varrho} _ {1} \leq c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {1} a / \gamma_ {1}.
$$

thus, the claim holds in the base case.

for the inductive step, fix some epoch  $m > 1$ . assume that for epoch  $m - 1$ , all rounds  $t'$  in epoch  $m - 1$  and all  $\pi \in \psi_{m - 1}$ ,

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq 2 \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t ^ {\prime}} (\pi) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a / \gamma_ {m - 1}, \tag {53}
$$

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t ^ {\prime}} (\pi) \leq 2 \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) + c _ {1} \varrho_ {m - 1} a / \gamma_ {m - 1}. \tag {54}
$$

we first show that for all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$  and all  $\pi \in \psi_{m}$

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq 2 \widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

for any  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) - \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i)}} {=} \left(\mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\operatorname {d i s}} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\operatorname {d i s}} (\pi)\right) - \left(\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\operatorname {d i s}} \left(\widehat {\pi} _ {m (t)}\right) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\operatorname {d i s}} (\pi)\right) \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i)}} {\leq} \left(\mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f ^ {*}}) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) - \left(\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f ^ {*}}) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i i)}} {\leq} \left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) \right| + \left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) - \mathcal {r} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) \right| \\ \stackrel {\text {(i v)}} {\leq} 4 c \sqrt {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right]} \frac {\lambda_ {m} \sqrt {a}}{\gamma_ {m}} + 4 c \sqrt {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \mid x)} \right]} \frac {\lambda_ {m} \sqrt {a}}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ \stackrel {(v)} {\leq} \left(\frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} + \frac {2 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}\right) + \left(\frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} + \frac {2 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}\right) \\ = \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} + \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {*}} (x) | x\right)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} + \frac {4 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}, \tag {55} \\ \end{array}
$$

where (i) is by lemma c.7, (ii) is by  $\pi_{f^{\star}}\in \psi_{m}$  and the optimality of  $\widehat{\pi}_m(\cdot)$  for  $\widehat{\mathcal{r}}_t(\cdot)$  over  $\psi_{m}$ , (iii) is by the triangle inequality, (iv) is by lemma c.8, and (v) is by the am-gm inequality. by (51) and  $\pi_{f^{\star}}\in \psi_{m - 1}$ ,

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \left\{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \right\}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} (\pi),
$$

and

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \leq q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right).
$$

combining the above two inequalities with  $q_{m - 1}\leq \varrho_{m - 1}$  and (54), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \leq \frac {q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} (\pi)}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ \leq \frac {\varrho_ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} (2 \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a / \gamma_ {m - 1})}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ = \frac {\left(\varrho_ {m - 1} + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}\right) a}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} + \frac {2 \gamma_ {m - 1} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi)}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ = \left(\frac {\varrho_ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + c _ {1}\right) \frac {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}} + \frac {2}{5} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \frac {\gamma_ {m - 1} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ \stackrel {(i)} {\leq} \frac {(1 . 5 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho_ {m}} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}} + \frac {2}{5} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi), \tag {56} \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \leq \frac {q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}})}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ \leq \frac {\varrho_ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} (2 \operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}) + c _ {1} \varrho_ {m - 1} a / \gamma_ {m - 1})}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ \stackrel {\mathrm {(i i)}} {=} \frac {\left(\varrho_ {m - 1} + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}\right) a}{t \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \\ = \left(\frac {\varrho_ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + c _ {1}\right) \frac {\widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}} \\ \stackrel {\text {(i i i)}} {\leq} \frac {(1 . 5 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}}, \tag {57} \\ \end{array}
$$

where (i) in (56) follows from (44) and lemma c.9, (ii) in (57) follows from  $\mathrm{reg}(\pi_{f^{\star}}) = 0$ , and (iii) in (57) follows from (44). combining (55), (56) and (57), we have

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq \frac {7}{5} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) + \left(\frac {2 0 0 c ^ {2} + 2 c _ {1} + 3}{3}\right) \frac {q _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}} \leq 2 \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) + \frac {c _ {1} q _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}. \tag {58}
$$

we now show that for all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$  and all  $\pi \in \psi_{m}$

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) \leq 2 \operatorname {r e g} _ {t} (\pi) + c _ {1} \varrho_ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

similar to (55), for any round  $t$  in epoch  $m$  we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \widehat {\mathrm {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) - \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) \\ = \left(\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) - \left(\mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f \star}) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) \\ \leq \left(\widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) - \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) - \left(\mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi)\right) \\ \leq \left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi) \right| + \left| \widehat {\mathcal {r}} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) - \mathcal {r} _ {t} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) \right| \\ \leq 4 c \sqrt {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right]} \frac {\lambda_ {m} \sqrt {a}}{\gamma_ {m}} + 4 c \sqrt {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) \mid x)} \right]} \frac {\lambda_ {m} \sqrt {a}}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ \leq \left(\frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + \frac {2 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}\right) + \left(\frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + \frac {2 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}\right) \\ = \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} + \frac {4 0 c ^ {2} a}{\gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}}. \tag {59} \\ \end{array}
$$

by (51), we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) | x)} \right] \leq q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\mathrm {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}).
$$

hence, using (54), lemma c.9, (58), and  $\widehat{\mathrm{reg}}_t(\widehat{\pi}_m) = 0$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{| a (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) | > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m} (x) | x)} \right]}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} m} \leq \frac {q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m})}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {\varrho_ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} (2 \mathrm {r e g} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a / \gamma_ {m - 1})}{5 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} / \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {(1 . 5 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}} + \frac {2}{5} \operatorname {r e g} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) \\ \leq \frac {(1 . 5 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}} + \frac {2}{5} \left(2 \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\widehat {\pi} _ {m}) + \frac {c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \\ = \frac {\left(1 . 5 + 3 c _ {1}\right) \varrho_ {m} a}{5 \gamma_ {m}}. \tag {60} \\ \end{array}
$$

combining (56), (59) and (60), we have

$$
\widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) \leq \frac {7}{5} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) + \left(4 0 c ^ {2} + \frac {3 + 4 c _ {1}}{5}\right) \frac {q _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}} \leq 2 \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) + \frac {c _ {1} q _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}.
$$

this completes the inductive step, and we conclude that the claim is true for all  $m \in \mathbb{n}$ .

lemma c.11. assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold, and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . for all epochs  $m \in \mathbb{n}$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and all predictors  $f \in \mathcal{f}_m$ ,

$$
\operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right) \leq \left(2 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 1\right) c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

proof. we rewrite  $\operatorname{reg}(\pi_f)$  as  $\mathbb{e}_x[\mathbb{i}\{\pi_f(x) \neq \pi_{f^{\star}}(x)\}(f^{\star}(x, \pi_{f^{\star}}(x)) - f^{\star}(x, \pi_f(x))]$ , and we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \left. (\operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right)) ^ {2} \leq \left(\mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \mathbb {i} \left\{\pi_ {f} (x) \neq \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \right\} \left(\left| f (x, \pi_ {f} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f} (x)) \right| + \left| f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star} (x)}) - f (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)) \right|\right) \right]\right) ^ {2} \right. \right. \\ \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \left\{\pi_ {f} (x) \neq \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \right\}}{p _ {m - 1} (\pi_ {f} (x) \mid x)} + \frac {\mathbb {i} \left\{\pi_ {f} (x) \neq \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \right\}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}, a \sim p _ {m - 1} (\cdot | x)} \left[ (f ^ {\star} (x, a) - f (x, a) ^ {2} \right] \\ \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f} (x) \mid x\right)} + \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {*}} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \frac {(2 \beta_ {m} + c _ {\delta})}{n _ {m} / 2} \\ \leq \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f} (x) \mid x\right)} + \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \frac {1 6 \left(4 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 2\right) c ^ {2} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} ^ {2} a}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} ^ {2}}, \tag {61} \\ \end{array}
$$

where in the third inequality we use the fact that both  $\pi_f$  and  $\pi_{f^{\star}}$  belongs to  $\psi_{m - 1}$ , as well as lemma c.2. by (44), lemma c.10, (51), and the fact that round  $t_{m - 1}$  belongs to epoch  $m - 1$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \leq q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} \left(\pi_ {f}\right) \\ \leq \varrho_ {m - 1} a + 2 \gamma_ {m - 1} \operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a \\ \leq 3 / 2 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a + 2 \gamma_ {m - 1} \operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} _ {x} \left[ \frac {\mathbb {i} \{\left| \mathcal {a} (x ; \mathcal {f} _ {m - 1}) \right| > 1 \}}{p _ {m - 1} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x) \mid x\right)} \right] \leq q _ {m - 1} a + \gamma_ {m - 1} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t _ {m - 1}} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) \\ \leq \varrho_ {m - 1} a + 2 \gamma_ {m - 1} \operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}\right) + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a \\ = \varrho_ {m - 1} a + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a \\ \leq 3 / 2 \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a. \\ \end{array}
$$

plugging the above two inequalities into (61), we have

$$
\left(\mathrm {r e g} (\pi_ {f})\right) ^ {2} \leq (2 \gamma_ {m - 1} \mathrm {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) + (2 c _ {1} + 3) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} a) \frac {1 6 (4 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 2) c ^ {2} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} ^ {2} a}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \gamma_ {m} ^ {2}},
$$

which means that

$$
\left(\frac {\gamma_ {m}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m}} \operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right)\right) ^ {2} \leq 6 4 c ^ {2} \left(2 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 1\right) a \left(\frac {\gamma_ {m - 1}}{\widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1}} \operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right)\right) + 6 4 \left(c _ {1} + 1. 5\right) c ^ {2} \left(2 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 1\right) a ^ {2}. \tag {62}
$$

solving (62) for  $\operatorname{reg}(\pi_f)$ , we have

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) \leq (2 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 1) \left(3 2 c ^ {2} + 8 \sqrt {1 6 c ^ {4} + (c _ {1} + 1 . 5) c ^ {2}}\right) \frac {\varrho_ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}} \leq \frac {(2 \beta_ {m} / c _ {\delta} + 1) c _ {1} \varrho_ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}.
$$

corollary c.1. set  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ . assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold. define  $c_{2}\coloneqq (2m + 1)c_{1}$ . for all epochs  $m\in \mathbb{n}$ , all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ , and all predictors  $f\in \mathcal{f}_m$ ,

$$
\operatorname {r e g} \left(\pi_ {f}\right) \leq c _ {2} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

proof. this follows from lemma c.11 and part 3 of lemma c.2.  $\square$

remark. lemma c.8, lemma c.10, lemma c.11 require  $\mathcal{f}_m\subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . this can be relaxed to the following condition:  $\mathbb{i}\{|a(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1\}$  is non-increasing in  $m$  for all  $x\in \mathcal{x}$ .

# c.3.1 incorporating the policy disagreement coefficient

at this point, can bound the regret within each epoch using corollary c.1, which gives a bound in terms of the empirical disagreement probability  $\widehat{\varrho}_{m}$ . to proceed, we relate this quantity to the policy disagreement coefficient.

define

$$
\eta_ {m} := \sup  _ {f \in \mathcal {f} _ {m}} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f}),
$$

and let  $\operatorname{dis}(\pi') = \{x \mid \exists \pi \in \pi': \pi(x) \neq \pi^{\star}(x)\}$  for  $\pi' \subseteq \pi$ . of particular interest is

$$
\operatorname {d i s} \left(\pi_ {\eta_ {m}} ^ {\csc}\right) := \left\{x \mid \exists \pi \in \pi_ {\eta_ {m}} ^ {\csc}: \pi (x) \neq \pi^ {\star} (x) \right\}.
$$

we observe that for all  $m \in [m]$ , since  $\pi_f \in \pi_{\eta_m}^{\mathrm{csc}}$  for all  $f \in \mathcal{f}_m$ , we have

$$
q _ {m} = \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (| \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) | > 1) \leq \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (x \in \operatorname {d i s} (\pi_ {\eta_ {m}} ^ {\csc})).
$$

the following lemma uses this result to upper bound regret in terms of the disagreement coefficient.

lemma c.12. set  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  and  $\mu_{m} = 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ . assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold. then for all  $m\in [m]\setminus \{1\}$ , for all  $f\in \mathcal{f}_m$

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) \leq \frac {\eta_ {m - 1}}{\eta_ {m}} \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \eta_ {m - 1}) \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

proof. suppose  $q_{m-1} \geq \mu_{m-1}$ . then corollary c.1, eq. (44), and the fact that  $q_{m-1} \leq \mathbb{p}_{\mathcal{d}}(x \in \mathrm{dis}(\pi_{\eta_{m-1}}^{\mathrm{csc}}))$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) \cdot \eta_ {m} \leq \left(\frac {c _ {2} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \left(\frac {c _ {2} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \\ = \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \frac {c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {4}{3} \varrho_ {m - 1} \frac {c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ = \left(q _ {m - 1} + \mu_ {m - 1}\right) \frac {4 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (x \in \mathrm {d i s} (\pi_ {\eta_ {m - 1}} ^ {\csc})) \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

thus

$$
\mathrm {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) \leq \frac {\mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (x \in \mathrm {d i s} (\pi_ {\eta_ {m - 1}} ^ {\csc}))}{\eta_ {m}} \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \leq \frac {\eta_ {m - 1}}{\eta_ {m}} \pmb {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \eta_ {m - 1}) \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}},
$$

where the second inequality invokes the definition of the disagreement coefficient.

on the other hand, suppose  $q_{m-1} < \mu_{m-1}$ . since  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \mathcal{f}_{m-1}$  (by part 3 of lemma c.2) and since  $|f(x,a)| \leq 1$  for all  $f \in \mathcal{f}$ , for all  $f \in \mathcal{f}_m$  we have

$$
\mathrm {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) = \mathcal {r} _ {\tau_ {m - 1} + 1} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f ^ {\star}}) - \mathcal {r} _ {\tau_ {m - 1} + 1} ^ {\mathrm {d i s}} (\pi_ {f}) \leq q _ {m} \leq q _ {m - 1} <   \mu_ {m - 1} = \frac {6 4 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}} = \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

summing both cases, we have

$$
\operatorname {r e g} (\pi_ {f}) \leq \frac {\eta_ {m - 1}}{\eta_ {m}} \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \eta_ {m - 1}) \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

we now solve the recurrence in lemma c.12 to obtain an absolute upper bound on  $\eta_{m}$  for each round.

lemma c.13. set  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  and  $\mu_{m} = 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ . assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold. fix any  $\varepsilon >0$ . for every epoch  $m\in [m]$ , if  $\eta_{m} > \varepsilon$ , then

$$
\eta_ {m} \leq \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

proof. we prove this result by induction. the hypothesis trivially holds for  $m = 1$ . now assume that the hypothesis holds for  $m - 1$  where  $m > 1$ . if  $\eta_{m} \leq \varepsilon$  then we are done. if  $\eta_{m} > \varepsilon$ , then by part 3 of lemma c.2, we have  $\eta_{m - 1} \geq \eta_{m} > \varepsilon$ . we consider two cases.

case 1:  $\eta_{m} \geq \frac{1}{2}\eta_{m-1}$ . in this case, by lemma c.12 we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {m} \leq \frac {\eta_ {m - 1}}{\eta_ {m}} \theta^ {\csc} (\pi , \eta_ {m - 1}) \frac {8 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \eta_ {m - 1}) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

and by  $\eta_{m - 1}\geq \eta_m > \varepsilon$  , we have

$$
\eta_ {m} \leq \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

case 2:  $\eta_{m} < \frac{1}{2}\eta_{m - 1}$ . since  $\eta_{m - 1} \geq \eta_{m} > \varepsilon$ , by the induction assumption we have

$$
\eta_ {m - 1} \leq \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 2}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}},
$$

and by  $\eta_{m} < \frac{1}{2}\eta_{m - 1}$  we know that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {m} <   \frac {1}{2} \left(\boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 2}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}}\right) \\ = \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

where we use that fact that  $n_{m - 1} = 2n_{m - 2}$

combining case 1 and case 2, we have that the hypothesis holds for  $m$ , concluding the inductive proof.  $\square$

lemma c.14. assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold, and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . for every epoch  $m \in [m]$ ,

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq (3 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a / \gamma_ {m}.
$$

proof. fix any epoch  $m \in \mathbb{n}$ . since  $\tau_{m-1} + 1$  belongs to epoch  $m$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \left(\widehat {2 \operatorname {r e g}} _ {\tau_ {m - 1} + 1} (\pi) + \frac {c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \\ = 2 \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {\tau_ {m - 1} + 1} (\pi) + \frac {c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} 0 a}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ \leq \frac {\left(2 q _ {m} + c _ {1} \widehat {\varrho} _ {m}\right) a}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ \leq \frac {(3 + c _ {1}) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality follows from lemma c.10, the second inequality follows from (50), and the third inequality follows from  $q_{m} \leq \varrho_{m} \leq \frac{3}{2}\widehat{\varrho}_{m}$  by lemma c.3.

lemma c.15. set  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  and  $\mu_{m} = 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ . assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  hold. fix any  $\varepsilon >0$ . for every epoch  $m\in [m]$ ,

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) \leq \max \left\{\varepsilon , \pmb {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {3 2 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \right\} + \frac {2 5 6 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

proof. the result trivially holds for  $m = 1$ .

for  $m > 1$ , we consider several cases.

case 1:  $q_{m - 1}\geq \mu_{m - 1}$  . by lemma c.14, (44), and  $q_{m - 1}\leq \mathbb{p}_{\mathcal{d}}(x\in \mathrm{dis}(\pi_{\eta_{m - 1}}^{\mathrm{csc}}))$  , using the definition of  $\gamma_{m}$  we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi)\right) ^ {2} \leq \left(\frac {(c _ {1} + 3) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \left(\frac {(c _ {1} + 3) \widehat {\varrho} _ {m} a}{\gamma_ {m}}\right) \\ = \widehat {\varrho} _ {m - 1} \frac {(c _ {1} + 3) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \frac {4}{3} \varrho_ {m - 1} \frac {\left(c _ {1} + 3\right) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ = \left(q _ {m - 1} + \mu_ {m - 1}\right) \frac {4 \left(c _ {1} + 3\right) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq \mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} (x \in \mathrm {d i s} (\pi_ {\eta_ {m - 1}} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}})) \frac {8 (c _ {1} + 3) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

thus

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq \frac {\mathbb {p} _ {\mathcal {d}} \left(x \in \operatorname {d i s} \left(\pi_ {\eta_ {m - 1}} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}}\right)\right)}{\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi)} \frac {8 \left(c _ {1} + 3\right) ^ {2} a \log \left(2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta\right)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}}. \tag {63}
$$

case 1.1:  $\sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_m(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi) > \eta_{m - 1}$ . in this case,  $\pi_{\eta_{m - 1}}^{\mathsf{csc}}\subset \pi_{r_m}^{\mathsf{csc}}$ , where  $r_m\coloneqq \sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_m(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi)$ , and (63) implies that

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) \leq \pmb {\theta} ^ {\csc} \left(\pi , r _ {m}\right) \frac {8 (c _ {1} + 3) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

if  $\sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_m(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi) > \varepsilon$  , then

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) <   \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {8 (c _ {1} + 3) ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

otherwise,  $\sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_m(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi)\leq \varepsilon$

case 1.2:  $\sum_{\pi \in \psi}q_m(\pi)\mathrm{reg}(\pi)\leq \eta_{m - 1}$ . in this case, we apply lemma c.13, which gives

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) \leq \eta_ {m - 1} \leq \max \left\{\varepsilon , \pmb {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 2}} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}} \right\}.
$$

case 2:  $q_{m-1} < \mu_{m-1}$ . by part 3 of lemma c.2 and lemma c.7, and using that  $|f^{\star}(x,a)| \leq 1$ , we have

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) = \sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq q _ {m} \leq q _ {m - 1} <   \mu_ {m - 1} = \frac {6 4 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}}.
$$

combining all the cases above, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \mathrm {r e g} (\pi) \leq \max  \left\{\varepsilon , \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {1 6 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 2}} \right\} + \frac {1 2 8 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 2}} \\ = \max \left\{\varepsilon , \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {3 2 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \right\} + \frac {2 5 6 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/eeb8af4f5de023c53a691080a2248f86e0995b39b2549ad2b12f1cc8d20ed87b.jpg)

lemma c.16. for any  $\delta \in (0,1]$ ,  $c > 0$ , by setting  $\beta_{m} = (m - m + 1)c_{\delta}$  and  $\mu_{m} = 64\log (4m / \delta) / n_{m - 1}$  for all  $m\in [m]$ , algorithm 1 with option i ensures that for every instance,

$$
\overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (c ^ {2} + c ^ {- 2}) \cdot \min  _ {\varepsilon > 0} \max  \left\{\varepsilon t, \theta^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) a \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta) \right\} + \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (\log (1 / \delta))
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta$

proof. by lemma c.1, lemma c.2 and lemma c.3, the choice of  $\{\beta_m\}_{m=1}^m$  and  $\{\mu_m\}_{m=1}^m$  ensures that  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$  simultaneously hold with probability at least  $1 - \delta$ , and  $f^\star \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . by lemma c.15, conditional on the occurrence of  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{dp}}$ , for any  $\varepsilon > 0$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} = \sum_ {t = 1} ^ {t} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \\ \leq \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} \sum_ {t = \tau_ {m - 1} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m}} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \right] \\ = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} \sum_ {t = \tau_ {m - 1} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m}} \sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \\ \leq \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} n _ {m} \cdot \left(\max  \left\{\varepsilon , \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {3 2 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2} n _ {m - 1}} \right\} + \frac {2 5 6 \log (4 m / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}\right) \\ = \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} \left(\max  \left\{\varepsilon n _ {m}, \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\mathbf {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {6 4 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2}} \right\} + 5 1 2 \log (4 m / \delta)\right) \\ \leq m \cdot \max  \left\{2 \varepsilon t, \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) \frac {6 4 c _ {2} ^ {2} a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{3 c ^ {2}} \right\} + 5 1 2 m \log (4 m / \delta) \\ = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(c _ {2} ^ {2} / c ^ {2}\right) \cdot \max  \left\{\varepsilon t, \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\csc} (\pi , \varepsilon) a \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta) \right\} + \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (\log (1 / \delta)) \\ = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (c ^ {2} + c ^ {- 2}) \cdot \max \left\{\varepsilon t, \pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) a \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta) \right\} + \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (\log (1 / \delta)). \\ \end{array}
$$

thus

$$
\overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (c ^ {2} + c ^ {- 2}) \cdot \min  _ {\varepsilon > 0} \max  \left\{\varepsilon t, \theta^ {\mathrm {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) a \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta) \right\} + \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (\log (1 / \delta))
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta$

proof of theorem 2.1. the  $\theta^{\mathrm{csc}}$ -based upper bound in theorem 2.1 can be directly obtained from lemma c.16: by taking  $\delta = 1 / t$  and  $c = 1$ , we have

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ \operatorname {r e g} _ {t} \right] = \mathbb {e} \left[ \overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} \right] = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (1) \cdot \min  _ {\varepsilon > 0} \max  \left\{\varepsilon t, \boldsymbol {\theta} ^ {\text {c s c}} (\pi , \varepsilon) a \log | \mathcal {f} | \right\} + \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (1).
$$

the  $\pmb{\theta}^{\mathrm{pol}}$ -based upper bound (under the uniform gap assumption) in theorem 2.1 is an immediate corollary of the  $\pmb{\theta}^{\mathrm{csc}}$ -based upper bound.

# c.4 proof of theorem 2.3

we now prove that adacb with option ii, i.e. with

$$
\lambda_ {m} = \mathbb {i} \left\{\widehat {w} _ {m} \geq \frac {\sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}} \right\}
$$

for all  $m\in [m]$ , attains the regret bound in theorem 2.3.

lemma c.17. assume that  $\mathcal{e}$  holds and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m$  for all  $m \in [m]$ . then for all epochs  $m \in [m]$  and all rounds  $t$  in epoch  $m$ ,

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \leq 2 w _ {m}.
$$

proof. consider any epoch  $m \in [m]$ , any round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ . we have

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}, a \sim p _ {m} (\cdot | x)} \left[ f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi^ {\star} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) \right]
$$

for all  $x$  such that  $|\mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| = 1$ , since  $f^{\star}\in \mathcal{f}_m$ , we have  $p_m(\pi^\star (x)|x) = 1$  and  $w(x;\mathcal{f}_m) = 0$ , thus

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {a \sim p _ {m} (\cdot | x)} [ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) | x ] = 0 = 2 w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}).
$$

for all  $x$  such that  $|\mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1$ , for all  $a \in \mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)$ , there exists  $f^\circ \in \mathcal{f}_m$  such that  $\pi_{f^\circ}(x) = a$ , thus

$$
\begin{array}{l} f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) = f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)) \\ \leq f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) + f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) \\ = \left| f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) \right| + \left| f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) \right| \\ \leq 2 \sup  _ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} \sup  _ {f, f ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {f} _ {m}} | f (x, a) - f ^ {\prime} (x, a) | \\ = 2 w (x, \mathcal {f} _ {m}). \\ \end{array}
$$

hence for all  $x$  such that  $|\mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1$ ,

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {a \sim p _ {m} (\cdot | x)} [ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) \mid x ] \leq \sup  _ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} [ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) ] \leq 2 w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}).
$$

putting both cases together, we have

$$
\mathbb {e} _ {a \sim p _ {m} (\cdot | x)} [ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) \mid x ] \leq 2 w (x. \mathcal {f} _ {m}), \quad \forall x \in \mathcal {x}
$$

and

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} (\pi^ {\star} (x _ {t})) - r _ {t} (a _ {t}) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] = \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}, a \sim p _ {m} (\cdot | x)} \left[ f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) \right] \leq 2 \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ w (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m}) \right] = 2 w _ {m}.
$$

# c.4.1 minimax regret

before we derive sharp instance-dependent guarantees for adacb with option ii, we first show that option ii will never degrade the algorithm's worst-case performance, i.e., adacb with option ii always guarantees the minimax rate  $\widetilde{\mathcal{o}} (\sqrt{at\log|\mathcal{f}|})$

corollary c.2. for all epoch  $m \in [m]$ , all round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ ,  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  is a feasible solution to:

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi_ {m}} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi) \leq a / \gamma_ {m},
$$

$$
\forall \pi \in \psi_ {m}, \quad \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq a + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi). \tag {64}
$$

proof. this is a direct corollary of lemma c.5.  $\square$

lemma c.18. for any epoch  $m \in [m]$ , if  $\gamma_m > 0$ , then  $\gamma_m \geq \max \{\gamma_1, \dots, \gamma_{m-1}\}$ .

proof. if  $\gamma_{m} > 0$ , then  $\lambda_{m} = 1$ , thus  $\gamma_{m} = c\sqrt{(an_{m - 1}) / \log(2|\mathcal{f}|t^{2}) / \delta}$ . for any  $m' \in \{1, \dots, m - 1\}$ ,

$$
\gamma_ {m ^ {\prime}} = \lambda_ {m ^ {\prime}} \cdot c \sqrt {\frac {a n _ {m ^ {\prime} - 1}}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}} \leq c \sqrt {\frac {a n _ {m ^ {\prime} - 1}}{\log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}} \leq \gamma_ {m}.
$$

lemma c.19. assume that  $\mathcal{e}$  holds, and  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m$  for all  $m \in [m]$ . let  $c_1 \coloneqq 200c^2 + 3$ . for all epochs  $m \in [m]$  such that  $\gamma_m > 0$ ,

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \leq \frac {(2 + c _ {1}) a}{\gamma_ {m}}.
$$

proof. the proof is based on slight modifications of lemma 7, lemma 8 and lemma 9 of simchi-levi and xu (2020), which (essentially) proves the same result based on the following two conditions

1.  $q_{m}(\cdot)$  is a feasible solution to

$$
\sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \widehat {\mathrm {r e g} _ {t}} (\pi) \leq a / \gamma_ {m},
$$

$$
\forall \pi \in \psi , \quad \mathbb {e} _ {x \sim \mathcal {d}} \left[ \frac {1}{p _ {m} (\pi (x) \mid x)} \right] \leq a + \gamma_ {m} \widehat {\operatorname {r e g}} _ {t} (\pi). \tag {65}
$$

2. for any epoch  $m\in [m]$ $\gamma_{m}\geq \max \{\gamma_{1},\ldots ,\gamma_{m - 1}\}$

while our corollary c.2 is weaker than their first condition (specifically, (64) holds for  $\forall \pi \in \psi_{m}$  while (65) requires  $\forall \pi \in \psi$ ), their proof still works under our condition, as we have assumed that  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_{m}$  for all  $m \in [m]$ . while our lemma c.18 is weaker than their second condition, this difference does not affect lemma c.19, as lemma c.19 only considers epochs  $m$  such that  $\gamma_{m} > 0$ . as a result, lemma c.19 is indeed implied by their result.

lemma c.20. for any  $\delta \in (t^{-2},1]$ ,  $c > 0$ , by setting  $\beta_{m} \geq c_{\delta} / 2$  for all  $m \in [m]$ , algorithm 1 with option ii ensures that for every instance,

$$
\overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (c + c ^ {- 1}) \cdot \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}
$$

with probability at least  $1 - \delta$ .

proof. without loss of generality, we assume that  $t$  is sufficiently large such that  $3/2\sqrt{at\log(|\mathcal{f}|/\delta)} \geq 64\log(4m/\delta)$  for all  $\delta \in (t^{-2},1]$  (otherwise,  $t$  must be upper bounded by an absolute constant, and we know that  $\overline{\mathrm{reg}}_t$  is upper bounded by the same constant).

by lemma c.1 and lemma c.4,  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}$  simultaneously hold with probability at least  $1 - \delta$ . in the rest of the proof, we assume that both  $\mathcal{e}$  and  $\mathcal{e}_{\mathrm{w}}$  hold. by lemma c.2, the specification of  $\{\beta_m\}_{m=1}^t$  in algorithm 1 ensures that  $f^\star \in \mathcal{f}_m$  for  $m \in [m]$ .

consider any epoch  $m \in [m]$ , any round  $t$  in epoch  $m$ . when  $w_m < \frac{3\sqrt{at\log(|\mathcal{f}| / \delta)}}{2n_{m - 1}}$ , by lemma c.17,

$$
\mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \leq 2 w _ {m} = \frac {3 \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}}. \tag {66}
$$

when  $w_{m}\geq \frac{3\sqrt{at\log(|\mathcal{f}| / \delta)}}{2n_{m - 1}}$  we have  $w_{m}\geq \frac{3\sqrt{at\log(|\mathcal{f}| / \delta)}}{2n_{m - 1}}\geq \frac{64\log(4m / \delta)}{n_{m - 1}}$  and thus by (46),

$$
\widehat {w} _ {m} \geq \frac {2}{3} w _ {m} \geq \frac {\sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}},
$$

and we have

$$
\lambda_ {m} = \mathbb {i} \left\{\widehat {w} _ {m} \geq \frac {\sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}} \right\} = 1,
$$

which implies  $\gamma_{m} > 0$ . by lemma c.19, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] = \sum_ {\pi \in \psi} q _ {m} (\pi) \operatorname {r e g} (\pi) \\ \leq \frac {(2 + c _ {1}) a}{\gamma_ {m}} \\ = (2 0 0 c + 3 c ^ {- 1}) \sqrt {\frac {a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}}. \tag {67} \\ \end{array}
$$

combining (66) and (67), we have

$$
\mathbb {e} [ r _ {t} (\pi^ {\star} (x _ {t})) - r _ {t} (a _ {t}) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} ] \leq \frac {3 \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}} + (2 0 0 c + 3 c ^ {- 1}) \sqrt {\frac {a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}}.
$$

therefore,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \overline {{\operatorname {r e g}}} _ {t} = \sum_ {t = 1} ^ {t} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {t - 1} \right] \\ \leq \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} \sum_ {t = \tau_ {m - 1} + 1} ^ {\tau_ {m}} \mathbb {e} \left[ r _ {t} \left(\pi^ {\star} \left(x _ {t}\right)\right) - r _ {t} \left(a _ {t}\right) \mid \mathfrak {g} _ {\tau_ {m - 1}} \right] \\ \leq \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} n _ {m} \cdot \left(\frac {3 \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}}{n _ {m - 1}} + (2 0 0 c + 3 c ^ {- 1}) \sqrt {\frac {a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)}{n _ {m - 1}}}\right) \\ = 6 m \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)} + (4 0 0 c + 6 c ^ {- 1}) \sqrt {a \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)} \sum_ {m = 1} ^ {m} \sqrt {n _ {m - 1}} \\ \leq 6 m \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)} + (4 0 0 c + 6 c ^ {- 1}) m \sqrt {a t \log (2 | \mathcal {f} | t ^ {2} / \delta)} \\ = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} (c + c ^ {- 1}) \cdot \sqrt {a t \log (| \mathcal {f} | / \delta)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

# c.4.2 instance-dependent regret

we now show that whenever the uniform gap condition

$$
f ^ {\star} (x, \pi^ {\star} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) \geq \delta \quad \forall a \neq \pi^ {\star} (x), \forall x \in \mathcal {x}
$$

holds, adacb with option ii enjoys the  $\frac{\theta^{\mathrm{val}}\log|\mathcal{f}|}{\delta}$ -type instance-dependent rate in theorem 2.3.

lemma c.21. assume  $\mathcal{e}$  holds and that  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m \subset \dots \subset \mathcal{f}_1$ . for all  $m > 1$ , for all choices for  $\beta_m \geq 0$ , it holds that

$$
w _ {m} \leq 4 (a + \gamma_ {m - 1}) \cdot \frac {\pmb {\theta} ^ {\mathrm {v a l}} \left(\mathcal {f} , \delta / 2 , \sqrt {(2 \beta_ {m} + c _ {\delta}) / n _ {m - 1}}\right)}{\delta} \cdot \frac {2 \beta_ {m} + c _ {\delta}}{n _ {m - 1}}.
$$

proof. consider any epoch  $m > 1$ . define  $\varepsilon_{m} = \sqrt{2\beta_{m} + c_{\delta}}$ . we first observe that for all  $x$  such that  $|\mathcal{a}(x; \mathcal{f}_{m})| > 1$ , there exists  $f^{\circ} \in \mathcal{f}_{m}$  such that  $\pi_{f^{\circ}}(x) \neq \pi_{f^{\star}}(x)$  and

$$
f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)) \geq \delta .
$$

thus

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta \leq f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)) - f ^ {\star} (x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)) \\ \leq f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) + f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) \\ = \left| f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\circ}} (x)\right) \right| + \left| f ^ {\circ} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) - f ^ {\star} \left(x, \pi_ {f ^ {\star}} (x)\right) \right| \\ \leq 2 \sup  _ {a \in \mathcal {a} (x; \mathcal {f} _ {m})} \sup  _ {f \in \mathcal {f} _ {m}} | f (x, a) - f ^ {\star} (x, a) |, \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality utilizes  $f^{\star} \in \mathcal{f}_m$ .

therefore,  $\sup_{a\in \mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)}\sup_{f'\in \mathcal{f}_m}|f(x,a) - f^\star (x,a)|\geq \delta /2$  whenever  $|\mathcal{a}(x;\mathcal{f}_m)| > 1$ . we then have

$$
